had to run away from.","At four, the museum called.","Mara and Cassian returned in a cab whose radio discussed the city's new water tariff. Neither listened. On the seat between them lay Reed's photograph, protected by a clear sleeve.","The registrar met them with a narrow envelope. Inside was a copy of the appendix and a short note written on the back of an old delivery receipt. Reed had left it with Penn's request for the record, to be given to whoever verified the flower's history.","The handwriting matched the letter at the theatre. Leda confirmed the particular way he crossed his final letters. That supported the connection; no one pretended it settled every question about his movements.","Mara unfolded the receipt.","Asterfall Academy of Conservation.","Fourteen Lantern Rise.","West research wing. Room Seventeen.","Below the address, Reed had added: They taught me to restore a recording there. The bench remembers what I cannot. Don't turn it on.","Mara looked at the workbench photograph. The drawer had a pale stripe of paint across one leg and a cable coiled on top. Nothing in the picture proved what had happened there. It did give them a place to ask a question that could be checked.","\"Is the academy open?\" she asked.","\"To students and qualified fellows,\" the registrar said. \"The old research rooms are restricted. You can apply for a supervised visit.\"","\"And if they refuse?\"","Penn slid a brochure across the table. The Academy Fellowship competition accepted experienced restorers without conventional qualifications, provided they passed an independent assessment. Successful applicants received access to specialist records during the preliminary stage. Further privileges depended on demonstrated competence and the college's rules.","The application required a record of work, a condition report, and references. A title could not replace the practical assessment. Mara found herself relieved by the rule, though she knew that a formal rule did not guarantee an impartial decision.","\"I can provide a reference,\" Penn offered.","\"For the repair you saw,\" she said.","\"For that.\"","Cassian looked at the form but did not offer to write to the dean. She liked his restraint enough to be irritated by her own pleasure in it. An assistant who knew when to remain an assistant might be more dangerous to her peace than a duke.","Mara examined the page. It offered no guarantee that she could enter Room Seventeen. It offered a legitimate route to being close enough to ask again.","Outside, rain had stopped. A thin line of light lay across the museum steps. Cassian asked where she wanted to go.","\"The academy,\" she said.","\"Today?\"","\"Today I want to look at the gate. Tomorrow I can decide how to enter it.\"","He opened the cab door, then paused. \"Would you like an assistant?\"","She looked at his immaculate coat. \"Can you take instructions?\"","\"Under appropriate supervision.\"","\"Can you make tea?\"","\"I am improving.\"","\"Can you refrain from becoming engaged to anyone during a practical assessment?\"","His smile arrived slowly. \"I may need a written condition.\"","Mara held out her hand. He took it, lightly, allowing her to decide how long the contact lasted.","\"A temporary appointment,\" she said.","\"Of course.\"","She climbed into the cab and left a place for him beside her. In her pocket, the flower rested against the folded address. Reed was still missing. Her mother's history was still incomplete. The city had not become kinder because they had learned to name a few of its wrongs.","But this time, when Mara went looking for a locked door, she had someone who would hold the light while she chose the fellowship brochure matter to Mara at the end of the volume?</strong></p><div class=\"options\"><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"0\">A. It confirms that Reed is currently living in the academy.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"1\">B. It gives Cassian authority to waive the practical assessment.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"2\">C. It establishes that Elena originally made the whole silver mechanism.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"3\">D. It offers a legitimate route towards restricted records without guaranteeing access to the room.</button></div><div class=\"feedback\" hidden=\"hidden\"></div></div></section><nav class=\"chapter-nav\" aria-label=\"Chapter navigation\"><button data-go=\"10\">← Previous</button><button data-go=\"12\">Next →</button></nav></article>"},{"id":"v02c01","title":"The Price of Admission","volume":"卷二｜谎言学院","anchor":"chapter-0013","paragraphs":["The address on the missing witness's note belonged to a college that had once refused to employ Mara because she lacked a degree. Its granite steps were now covered by a crimson carpet, and a crow was trying to acquire a sandwich from a man in a ceremonial coat. Neither the carpet nor the man appeared equal to the occasion.","\"A promising institution,\" Mara said. \"The security already knows whom to rob.\"","Cassian rescued the sandwich before the bird could carry it off. He handed it back without explaining that a duke had just negotiated with a crow. \"We can use the side entrance.\"","\"You can. My application specifically says the general public must arrive at the front.\"","The witness had left a room number as well as an address. Room Seventeen was located in the old research wing, beyond a door that opened solely to staff and fellowship holders. Mara had spent the previous evening looking for another route. The basement had an alarm, the roof was visible from the guardhouse, and a lorry carrying catering supplies stopped outside a gate monitored by two cameras. An invitation was less exciting than a burglary, but it was also less likely to end with a footstep on her neck.","The Academy Fellowship offered the legitimate route. Each year, on the anniversary of the college's foundation, experienced restorers competed to repair an object from the city's collection. The winner received a grant and access to specialised records. Applicants without academic credentials could take an independent assessment. In theory, individual worth mattered more than the name of one's school. Mara intended to investigate how much work those two words were doing.","At the registration desk, a sophomore volunteer read her form, looked at Cassian, and read it again. \"Your sponsor can accompany you to the auditorium.\"","\"He isn't my sponsor.\"","\"He's your fiancé.\"","\"An ongoing administrative problem.\"","Cassian's mouth moved. It was not quite a smile. He placed a paper container on the desk. \"Breakfast. She considers food a form of interference. I thought you might interpret it differently.\"","The volunteer accepted the cheese pastry with joy and gave Mara a blue admission card. \"The independent group meets in the west workshop. Professor Holt will select the final candidates. Be cautious of the third stair. It gives way.\"","The staircase turned out to be cast iron. Its third tread moved under Mara's weight, and Cassian caught the rail above her hand. He did not pull her back. He waited until she had found a smooth section of wall against which to brace herself, then steadied the tread with his shoe. A timely intervention, she thought, followed by the restraint to let her finish. It was alarmingly difficult to resent that combination.","Inside the workshop, a graduate student was adjusting a computer beside a mechanical bird. A pendulum swung behind its glass breast. The bird's wing had split along the border of an old repair, exposing a dull alloy beneath the silver. Mara recognized the brand on the stand. Her first employer had bought the same mechanism, second-rate copies intended to impress customers who could not afford the original.","A large woman in flour-dusted trousers looked up from a workbench. \"Independent applicants? Name and expertise. I'm Tamsin Holt. Before anyone asks, the flour belongs to my breakfast, not the collection.\"","Mara gave her name. \"Small mechanisms. Surface repair. The relationship between what an owner remembers and what an object actually does.\"","\"An abstract answer. Can you make it useful?\"","\"Your bird is being blamed for its stand.\"","Holt's expression remained non-committal. \"You have five minutes. Nobody touches it until they've explained the purpose of the test.\"","There were four other candidates. One was a school leaver with a wooden tool case. Another had taught mathematics at a charter school before a budget cut closed her department. An elder restorer from an inland town said his farm tools were more complicated than most things sold as priceless. The fourth applicant, wearing high fashion with the self-consciousness of someone hoping nobody noticed, introduced himself as Lucien Orr.","\"I have a recommendation from the University of Oxford,\" Lucien said. \"I hope we won't be judged solely on charming guesses.\"","\"Neither do I,\" said Mara.","Holt asked the applicants to count the wing's movements. The student recorded their figures while a projection repeated the sequence on the wall. After the third round, the school leaver ventured that acceleration in the pendulum indicated wear-and-tear. Lucien proposed a revised equation. The teacher noticed a discrepancy between the recorded image and the bird itself. Nobody appeared incompetent. Mara's gut told her the test had been designed to make competent people defend the wrong assumption.","She crouched beside the stand. \"May I see the holder without the bird?\"","\"Why?\" Holt asked.","\"Because the wing moves differently when the pendulum passes the middle. That could derive from the mechanism. It could also mean the base is twisting. An absence of damage in a cursory inspection doesn't establish that there is none.\"","\"And if the base is sound?\"","\"Then I learn a lesson and reevaluate.\"","Holt lifted the bird. Under its weight, the stand had pressed a broken pad against a pipe carrying the display cable. Each passing swing pulled the cable tight. Without the load, the loose piece lay flat. The damage was small enough to frustrate an inspection made from above and serious enough to have an impact on the result.","\"Not a miracle,\" Mara said, looking at Lucien. \"A supporting part. It rarely gets a recommendation.\"","The graduate student gave a cough that concealed laughter. Holt did not conceal hers. \"The first precondition of repair is to identify what needs repairing. The second is to resist the thrill of declaring yourself a genius. Miss Vale, explain how you would keep the stand stable without destroying the old fiber beneath it.\"","Mara answered carefully. She would attach a removable support, preserve the fragment, and test the repair twice under equal loads. She would not substitute a stronger material until she knew whether it would adversely affect the softer original. The aim was not conformity with a modern drawing but rather a sustainable repair that another person could understand and reverse.","Holt nodded. \"Permissible. Mr. Orr, do you agree?\"","\"With the method. I concede the finding.\"","The concession came stiffly, but it came. Mara liked him a little better for it.","Their next task was to paraphrase an old assessment. The paragraph praised a restoration campaign for its global importance while admitting that its statistical basis was uncertain. Holt asked whether a negative finding about one component justified calling the whole project a defeat. The school leaver said no. The teacher brought up the demographic information missing from the report. Lucien observed that the sample was too diverse to support the comparison. Mara pointed to the word \"may.\"","\"The writer is willing to theorize. She isn't claiming knowledge she doesn't have. If we remove that word, we change her meaning.\"","\"An invaluable habit,\" Holt said. \"Pay attention to the limits. They are part of the evidence.\"","Beyond the window, a builder was discussing plans to ceil the room above the workshop before winter. His colleague wanted a demolition order instead. Their argument drifted into the workshop and made the academy's prosperity sound considerably less secure. Mara glanced at the fine curtains. Somebody could fund a splendid public ceremony while leaving the roof unfunded. That phenomenon was familiar in its own right.","At noon, Holt read out the lineup. All five applicants had passed. The grant, she explained, was a donation administered by an independent board. Candidates would adhere to transparent rules, and anyone found guilty of deliberate interference would be removed. No name-and-shame spectacle was permissible before a fair investigation. She made the promise with the grimly patient air of someone who had needed it before.","\"One last question,\" Mara said. \"Does a fellowship holder have access to the west research wing?\"","\"After the preliminary round. Why?\"","\"I know of an old repair record that might help me identify a private object.\"","Holt glanced at the silver flower visible above Mara's collar. \"Bring it tomorrow. Records don't answer questions until someone asks a sensible one.\"","It was a step forward. It was also, Mara realized, an invitation to put the object she had stolen back into someone else's care. She had expected to struggle against a locked door. She had not expected the harder problem to be trust.","In the courtyard, Cassian was waiting beside a fountain with no water. A bronze Madonna stood in the shade beyond it, her upturned palms full of fallen leaves. Mara gave him the blue card. He studied it as though it were a medal.","\"You look amazed,\" she said.","\"I was thinking the academy has had a very good morning.\"","\"That is extravagant praise for an institution with a collapsing stair.\"","\"I wasn't praising the institution.\"","Her delight was disproportionate to the sentence. She put the card away before he could notice.","A marshal emerged from the auditorium carrying two copies of the competition rules. An assistant was required for the second round. The work was unpaid, the hours were unpleasant, and the assistant could not offer expert advice. Cassian took a copy before Mara could refuse on his behalf.","\"You ought to think about your temperament,\" she told him. \"Can you cope with being ordered about?\"","\"By you? I believe the probability is high.\"","\"And there is no godlike intervention. No frightening the chairman. No rescuing me from a result I deserve.\"","\"Whatever you decide.\"","She looked at the small print. \"You might get stuck cleaning tools.\"","\"Then I shall acquire a new skill.\"","Mara tried to maintain a severe expression. It was nuts to let a man with that face volunteer for dirty work. It was worse to realize that she wanted him there, not as a substitute for her courage, but as someone who would see what she could do."],"questions":[{"type":"inference","stem":"Why does Mara ask to one."],"answer":0,"explanation":"她观察到运动异常可能来自底座，而不是立即断定鸟本身损坏，所以要求分开检查；后文发现底座受力牵扯了电缆。","evidence":[26],"shuffled":true}],"wordCount":1666,"html":"<article class=\"chapter\" id=\"chapter-0013\" type=\"button\" data-option=\"0\">A. She wants to test whether an external support is affecting the result.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"1\">B. She has already proved that the bird is beyond repair.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"2\">C. She wants to avoid explaining a restoration method.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"3\">D. She believes a modern stand must always replace an old one.</button></div><div class=\"feedback\" hidden=\"hidden\"></div></div></section><nav class=\"chapter-nav\" aria-label=\"Chapter navigation\"><button data-go=\"11\">← Previous</button><button data-go=\"13\">Next →</button></nav></article>"},{"id":"v02c02","title":"The Assistant with the Wrong Hands","volume":"卷二｜谎言学院","anchor":"chapter-0014","paragraphs":["The academy provided an assistant's uniform in three sizes. Cassian tried the largest jacket, which was still too narrow across his shoulders. Mara watched him attempt to fasten it and experienced a deeply unprofessional degree of gratification.","\"You can laugh,\" he said.","\"I intend to. I'm waiting for the second button to surrender.\"","She found him a sturdy apron instead. Under the rules, assistants registered under their full legal names, but those details remained with the registrar until the judging was complete. Their visible badges showed numbers. Cassian became Assistant Eleven, a position whose mandate included carrying trays and obeying reasonable instructions. Mara intended to explore its educational possibilities.","Her assigned artefact occupied the center of a spacious worktable. It resembled a small theater built from painted wood, with a compact ticket mechanism beneath the stage. A procession of figures could move through an architectural landscape while a lamp cast their shadows on a celluloid screen. The catalogue described it as an automated nursery entertainment, designed to be operated by an adult. Somebody had crossed out the word \"nursery\" and added \"private demonstrations.\"","\"A toy with social ambitions,\" Mara said.","A woman at the adjoining table looked over. \"Most of the academy's patrons could be described that way. I'm Ada Shaw. If you need a spoon, I have six. If you need a screwdriver, I have none.\"","Ada was a part-time undergraduate who worked at the registry of a bankrupt theater. Her bread-and-butter work involved checking receipts, not restoring spectacles. She had taken a college-level course in materials conservation, but her practical credentials came from repairing things while a director shouted that the audience was already arriving.","\"A good place to learn,\" Mara said.","\"A terrible place to make a mistake. There are witnesses everywhere, on and off stage.\"","Professor Holt called for attention. Each contestant would prepare a chronological report, assess the object's condition, and identify one justified intervention. The curriculum offered optional seminars on computer programming and research methods. Those whose work crossed unfamiliar intellectual territories could ask for advice, but every contribution had to be declared. Plagiarism would void a submission. Admitting uncertainty would not.","\"Ideally,\" Holt added, \"you'll distinguish a reasoned inference from an attractive prediction. The first belongs in your report. The second may belong there too, provided you label it.\"","Lucien raised a gloved hand. \"May assistants interpret specialist texts?\"","\"They may read them aloud. They may not supply the interpretation. Their testimony must attest to what they did. If this distinction becomes unclear, ask before acting.\"","Mara looked meaningfully at Cassian. He looked down at his hands, folded them, and achieved an expression of such royal innocence that she nearly objected on principle.","The preliminary investigation began badly. Her mechanism would not turn. The paint was brittle, one support had started to crumble, and the lens magnified a dark shape that might have been a fracture. She was unprepared for the smell inside the case: warm dust, old wool, and something faintly sweet. An orange candy, forgotten by some previous visitor, had hardened against the gears.","\"I could fix this with a spoon,\" Ada said from her table.","\"Don't give him ideas.\"","Cassian set down the tray he was carrying. \"I was considering whether the candy counts as historical content.\"","\"Only if you can date it.\"","\"The smell suggests January. The state of it suggests the aftermath of a siege.\"","Mara took photographs before removing anything. A common hazard in restoration was the temptation to make an object look completely healthy before understanding what had happened to it. She could scrape out the candy in a moment. If she disturbed the little paper wrapper beneath it, however, she might destroy evidence of when the case had last been opened.","She asked Cassian to act as recorder. He entered the time and location while she lifted the wrapper with tweezers. Its logo belonged to a confectioner that had closed in February, three years earlier. The catalogue said the case had remained sealed for nine years. Those claims were at odds with each other. It was not yet clear which was wrong.","A conservator named Bellamy stopped behind them. \"That wrapper proves nothing. A volunteer may have opened the case to clean it.\"","\"Then the record needs an amendment,\" Mara said. \"I haven't accused anyone of anything.\"","\"A little knowledge can misguide an enthusiastic amateur.\"","She felt the familiar thrust of shame. The coat was borrowed. Her hands were marked by cheap chemicals. Here was a man whose signature could endow a questionable guess with authority, whereas hers would be examined for spelling.","Before she could answer, Cassian placed a clean sheet beside the report. He had written the catalogue reference and left the remainder blank. He was giving her somewhere to put her evidence. He was also resisting the conspicuous urge to remove Bellamy from the room by the collar.","Mara chose the paper. \"Would you initial the entry confirming that informal access was possible? It would help the next restorer.\"","Bellamy declined. Even so, he withdrew his objection to the photograph.","At lunch, they sat on a porch above the herb garden. Ada brought bread, and Mara divided a pie. Cassian tried to pour tea from a gigantic pot without using both hands. Mara handed him the second handle with the quiet zeal of someone watching a duke discover gravity.","\"What do you do when you aren't helping?\" Ada asked him.","\"Investigations.\"","\"Insurance?\"","\"Sometimes the consequences are similar.\"","Mara bit her bread to stop herself from laughing. Ada accepted the answer. She had no interest in fashionable faces and said she trusted people in accordance with what they did, a protocol that Mara found increasingly appealing.","The conversation turned to the competition. A team of consultants had proposed an algorithm to estimate the cost of repairing the academy's collection. Their model used the average time spent on a batch of modern objects. Ada wondered whether it was acceptable to apply that figure to a handmade mechanism whose parts were unique.","\"Statistically convenient,\" Mara said. \"Possibly deceptive.\"","Cassian reached for the last piece of pie. \"I am a rationalist. I require more evidence.\"","Ada pushed the empty plate toward him. \"The distribution is evidence-based. You ate two pieces already.\"","For a few minutes they were peaceful. No one mentioned the duke, a missing witness, or the reason Mara needed access to a particular room. The pleasure made her wary. Trustworthiness was not something she could divine from a shared lunch. Still, she had to start somewhere, and a woman willing to deny Cassian pie seemed a sound beginning.","Back in the workshop, Mara opened the device's lower compartment. A row of paper slots held punched tickets. Most bore stock numbers. One had been turned over. Across its blank side, in a hand so small that she needed the lens, somebody had written, \"The second catalogue has no names.\"","Her attention sharpened. The witness had sent her to the academy. Had he left another prompt here, or was she imposing an explanation on an unrelated fragment? She asked Cassian to photograph the ticket without moving it. He complied, but his hand paused when the camera brought the writing into focus.","\"You recognize it?\" she asked.","\"I recognize the abbreviation beside the date. It appeared in an accident file. That doesn't tell us who put it here.\"","The answer was careful. She preferred it to certainty.","She compared the numbered tickets with the printed catalogue. The sequence skipped from seventeen to nineteen. At first she thought one had fallen into the base. Then she noticed that the corresponding page had been removed from the digital service as well. An old link returned a warning. The record still existed somewhere; the public page no longer displayed it.","Mara asked for a copy. Bellamy told her to submit a request through the editor of the collection catalogue. The editor was absent until the following weekday. When she asked whether a backup could be consulted, he took a tough line. The restoration competition was not a license to rummage through private records.","\"Which are private?\" Mara asked. \"The catalogue describes this collection as public.\"","\"Some agreements predate its adoption by the academy.\"","\"May I read the agreement?\"","\"You may complete your assigned project.\"","She wanted to upend the table. Instead, she noted the refusal, recorded its time, and returned to the lens. Cassian made no unilateral attempt to intervene. He merely moved the lamp until the light fell where she needed it. The small kindness was more rewarding than a scene would have been.","By evening, the candy was in a labeled bag, the wrapper was safely enclosed, and the mechanism could move without grinding against the screen. Mara had not restored its function; she had only removed an obstruction. She made that limitation explicit. Bellamy might reject her interpretation, but he would have to argue with the distinction she had actually made.","Holt read the report, then checked the photographs. \"You have an eye for the gap between an object and its story. Don't let the story swallow the object.\"","\"I won't.\"","\"And don't backload your condition notes. Write them as you go. A clear-cut account tomorrow is of little use if it conceals your doubts today.\"","At the door, Ada caught Mara's sleeve. She looked unwilling to part with what she was about to say. \"The missing page. I've seen a copy of that catalogue at my theater. We used to serve as a storage site.\"","\"Could you bring it?\"","\"I can try. But beware of Bellamy. The last person who kept asking him for records didn't stay long.\"","Mara's tiredness receded. \"Did he leave?\"","Ada shook her head. \"That's the part nobody can agree on.\""],"questions":[{"type":"scope","stem":"What does the contradiction between the wrapper and the catalogue initially justify?","options":["Concluding that every academy record is false.","Accusing Bellamy of putting the candy inside.","Checking the record of when the case was opened.","Discarding the wrapper as worthless."],"answer":2,"explanation":"糖纸时间与封存记录不一致，只能支持核验开盒记录；正文明确说当时尚不清楚哪一项有误，不能据此认定谁造假。","evidence":[23],"shuffled":true}],"wordCount":1629,"html":"<article class=\"chapter\" id=\"chapter-0014\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><div class=\"kicker\">卷二｜谎言学院</div><h1>The Assistant with the Wrong Hands</h1><p id=\"chapter-0014-p1\"><a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-8385\" data-words=\"8385\">The</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-72\" data-words=\"72\">academy</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-6579\" data-words=\"6579,6577\">provided</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-362\" data-words=\"362\">an</a> assistant&#x27;s <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-8841\" data-words=\"8841\">uniform</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-4167\" data-words=\"4167\">in</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-8484\" data-words=\"8484\">three</a> <a class=\"vocab first\" href=\"#word-7609\" data-words=\"7609\">sizes</a>.
